                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">2</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">\r\n
                                        Qu&#039;est-ce que la profondeur de clic ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>C&#039;est le nombre minimum de clics nécessaires pour atteindre une page depuis l&#039;URL de départ. Les pages importantes devraient se trouver à trois clics ou moins : au-delà, elles sont explorées moins souvent et reçoivent moins d&#039;autorité interne.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">3</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">\r\n
                                        Comment l&#039;outil repère-t-il les pages orphelines ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Il lit le sitemap déclaré dans robots.txt (ou /sitemap.xml) et le compare aux pages trouvées en suivant les liens. Une URL présente dans le sitemap mais jamais atteinte par un lien est orpheline. Si l&#039;exploration a atteint la limite de pages, ces URL sont des candidates à vérifier : elles sont peut-être liées depuis une page non explorée.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">4</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">\r\n
                                        Pourquoi une page est-elle marquée « non indexable » ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Elle renvoie un code autre que 200, contient une directive noindex (balise meta robots ou en-tête X-Robots-Tag), déclare une URL canonique différente, ou n&#039;est pas une page HTML. Le panneau de détails indique la raison exacte.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">5</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">\r\n
                                        L&#039;exploration respecte-t-elle mon serveur ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Oui. Le robot s&#039;identifie comme CodeSommetBot, applique les règles de robots.txt, ne quitte jamais le domaine indiqué et ne demande que quatre pages à la fois. L&#039;exploration est limitée à 500 pages.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">6</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">\r\n
                                        Mon site affiche son contenu en JavaScript, est-ce un problème ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>L&#039;outil lit le HTML renvoyé par le serveur, comme le premier passage de Googlebot. Si vos liens n&#039;apparaissent qu&#039;après exécution du JavaScript, le graphe sera presque vide : c&#039;est un signal fort que votre site devrait proposer un rendu serveur.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n

    <script type="text/javascript">\n
    (function() {\n
        var SRC = "https://app.cal.eu/embed/embed.js";\n
        var started = false;\n
        var ready = false;\n
        var pendingClick = null;\n
\n
        function loadCal() {\n
            if (started) return;\n
            started = true;\n
\n
            (function(C, A, L) {\n
                let p = function(a, ar) {\n
                    a.q.push(ar);\n
                };\n
                let d = C.document;\n
                C.Cal = C.Cal || function() {\n
                    let cal = C.Cal;\n
                    let ar = arguments;\n
                    if (!cal.loaded) {\n
                        cal.ns = {};\n
                        cal.q = cal.q || [];\n
                        d.head.appendChild(d.createElement("script")).src = A;\n
                        cal.loaded = true;\n
                    }\n
                    if (ar[0] === L) {\n
                        const api = function() {\n
                            p(api, arguments);\n
                        };\n
                        const namespace = ar[1];\n
                        api.q = api.q || [];\n
                        if (typeof namespace === "string") {\n
                            cal.ns[namespace] = cal.ns[namespace] || api;\n
                            p(cal.ns[namespace], ar);\n
                            p(cal, ["initNamespace", namespace]);\n
                        } else p(cal, ar);\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    p(cal, ar);\n
                };\n
            })(window, SRC, "init");\n
            Cal("init", "new-client-meeting", {\n
                origin: "https://app.cal.eu"\n
            });\n
            Cal.ns["new-client-meeting"]("ui", {\n
                "hideEventTypeDetails": false,\n
                "layout": "month_view"\n
            });\n
\n
            var s = document.head.querySelector('script[src="' + SRC + '"]');\n
            if (s) {\n
                s.addEventListener('load', function() {\n
                    ready = true;\n
                    if (pendingClick) {\n
                        var btn = pendingClick;\n
                        pendingClick = null;\n
                        // Laisse l'embed scanner les data-cal-link, puis rejoue le clic.\n
                        setTimeout(function() {\n
                            btn.click();\n
                        }, 80);\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            } else {\n
                ready = true;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        function warm() {\n
            loadCal();\n
            ['pointerdown', 'touchstart', 'keydown', 'scroll'].forEach(function(ev) {\n
                window.removeEventListener(ev, warm);\n
            });\n
        }\n
        ['pointerdown', 'touchstart', 'keydown', 'scroll'].forEach(function(ev) {\n
            window.addEventListener(ev, warm, { passive: true });\n
        });\n
\n
        // Pré-chauffe au survol / focus d'un bouton de réservation.\n
        document.addEventListener('pointerover', function(e) {\n
            if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-cal-link]')) loadCal();\n
        }, { passive: true });\n
\n
        // Filet de sécurité : clic avant que l'embed soit prêt → on retient le\n
        // bouton et on rejoue le clic une fois embed.js chargé.\n
        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
            var btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-cal-link]');\n
            if (!btn || ready) return;\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            e.stopPropagation();\n
            pendingClick = btn;\n
            loadCal();\n
        }, true);\n
    })();\n
</script>\n\r

        (function() {\r\n
            var done = false;\r\n
\r\n
            function dismiss() {\r\n
                if (done) return;\r\n
                done = true;\r\n
                var p = document.getElementById('preloader');\r\n
                if (p) {\r\n
                    p.classList.add('loaded');\r\n
                    setTimeout(function() {\r\n
                        p.remove();\r\n
                    }, 600);\r\n
                }\r\n
            }\r\n
            // Ce script inline en fin de <body> s'exécute dès que le HTML est\r\n
            // parsé — AVANT le téléchargement des scripts defer (contrairement\r\n
            // à DOMContentLoaded, qui les attend : ~1,5 s de délai LCP en 4G\r\n
            // lente). Deux rAF = première opportunité de peinture, page déjà\r\n
            // entièrement stylée par le CSS critique inliné.\r\n
            requestAnimationFrame(function() {\r\n
                requestAnimationFrame(dismiss);\r\n
            });\r\n
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', dismiss);\r\n
            window.addEventListener('load', dismiss);\r\n
            setTimeout(dismiss, 2500);\r\n
        })();\r\n

                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Quelle différence entre JSON-LD, microdonnées et RDFa ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Ce sont trois syntaxes pour le même vocabulaire schema.org. JSON-LD est un bloc &lt;script&gt; indépendant du HTML, recommandé par Google ; les microdonnées (itemscope, itemprop) et le RDFa (typeof, property) s&#039;écrivent dans les balises. L&#039;outil valide le JSON-LD et signale la présence des deux autres.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">2</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Qu&#039;est-ce qu&#039;une propriété « requise » ou « recommandée » ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Google publie, pour chaque type de résultat enrichi (Article, Produit, FAQ, Événement…), les propriétés sans lesquelles le résultat n&#039;est pas éligible (requises) et celles qui l&#039;améliorent (recommandées). Une propriété requise manquante est une erreur ; une recommandée manquante, un avertissement.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">3</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Pourquoi mon bloc est-il « JSON invalide » ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Les causes classiques : une virgule après le dernier élément, des guillemets simples au lieu de doubles, un commentaire, ou du HTML non échappé dans une valeur. L&#039;outil indique si une virgule finale ou un commentaire suffisent à expliquer l&#039;erreur.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">4</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Un balisage valide garantit-il un résultat enrichi ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Non. Il le rend éligible. Google décide ensuite selon la qualité de la page, la cohérence entre le balisage et le contenu visible, et ses règles (les avis auto-attribués, par exemple, ne sont plus affichés).</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">5</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Faut-il un seul bloc ou plusieurs ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Les deux fonctionnent. Un seul bloc avec @graph permet de relier les entités entre elles (WebSite → Organization → WebPage → Article) grâce aux @id, ce que Google et les moteurs IA exploitent de mieux en mieux.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">6</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Quels types sont vérifiés ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Article et ses variantes, Product et Offer, Organization, LocalBusiness et ses sous-types, FAQPage, BreadcrumbList, WebSite, WebPage, Person, Event, Recipe, VideoObject, HowTo, JobPosting, Review, AggregateRating, SoftwareApplication, Service, Course, ImageObject… Les autres types sont extraits mais seulement contrôlés sur la forme.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                    </div>\r\n
                <div class="mt-6 text-center">\r\n
                    <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-2">Vous avez encore des questions ?</p><a href="/contact"\r\n

                                d="M17 8l4 4m0 0l-4 4m4-4H3"></path>\r\n
                        </svg></a>\r\n
                </div>\r\n
            </div>\r\n

    <script type="text/javascript">\n
    (function() {\n
        var SRC = "https://app.cal.eu/embed/embed.js";\n
        var started = false;\n
        var ready = false;\n
        var pendingClick = null;\n
\n
        function loadCal() {\n
            if (started) return;\n
            started = true;\n
\n
            (function(C, A, L) {\n
                let p = function(a, ar) {\n
                    a.q.push(ar);\n
                };\n
                let d = C.document;\n
                C.Cal = C.Cal || function() {\n
                    let cal = C.Cal;\n
                    let ar = arguments;\n
                    if (!cal.loaded) {\n
                        cal.ns = {};\n
                        cal.q = cal.q || [];\n
                        d.head.appendChild(d.createElement("script")).src = A;\n
                        cal.loaded = true;\n
                    }\n
                    if (ar[0] === L) {\n
                        const api = function() {\n
                            p(api, arguments);\n
                        };\n
                        const namespace = ar[1];\n
                        api.q = api.q || [];\n
                        if (typeof namespace === "string") {\n
                            cal.ns[namespace] = cal.ns[namespace] || api;\n
                            p(cal.ns[namespace], ar);\n
                            p(cal, ["initNamespace", namespace]);\n
                        } else p(cal, ar);\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    p(cal, ar);\n
                };\n
            })(window, SRC, "init");\n
            Cal("init", "new-client-meeting", {\n
                origin: "https://app.cal.eu"\n
            });\n
            Cal.ns["new-client-meeting"]("ui", {\n
                "hideEventTypeDetails": false,\n
                "layout": "month_view"\n
            });\n
\n
            var s = document.head.querySelector('script[src="' + SRC + '"]');\n
            if (s) {\n
                s.addEventListener('load', function() {\n
                    ready = true;\n
                    if (pendingClick) {\n
                        var btn = pendingClick;\n
                        pendingClick = null;\n
                        // Laisse l'embed scanner les data-cal-link, puis rejoue le clic.\n
                        setTimeout(function() {\n
                            btn.click();\n
                        }, 80);\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            } else {\n
                ready = true;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        function warm() {\n
            loadCal();\n
            ['pointerdown', 'touchstart', 'keydown', 'scroll'].forEach(function(ev) {\n
                window.removeEventListener(ev, warm);\n
            });\n
        }\n
        ['pointerdown', 'touchstart', 'keydown', 'scroll'].forEach(function(ev) {\n
            window.addEventListener(ev, warm, { passive: true });\n
        });\n
\n
        // Pré-chauffe au survol / focus d'un bouton de réservation.\n
        document.addEventListener('pointerover', function(e) {\n
            if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-cal-link]')) loadCal();\n
        }, { passive: true });\n
\n
        // Filet de sécurité : clic avant que l'embed soit prêt → on retient le\n
        // bouton et on rejoue le clic une fois embed.js chargé.\n
        document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n
            var btn = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-cal-link]');\n
            if (!btn || ready) return;\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            e.stopPropagation();\n
            pendingClick = btn;\n
            loadCal();\n
        }, true);\n
    })();\n
</script>\n\r

            function dismiss() {\r\n
                if (done) return;\r\n
                done = true;\r\n
                var p = document.getElementById('preloader');\r\n
                if (p) {\r\n
                    p.classList.add('loaded');\r\n
                    setTimeout(function() {\r\n
                        p.remove();\r\n
                    }, 600);\r\n
                }\r\n

            // Ce script inline en fin de <body> s'exécute dès que le HTML est\r\n
            // parsé — AVANT le téléchargement des scripts defer (contrairement\r\n
            // à DOMContentLoaded, qui les attend : ~1,5 s de délai LCP en 4G\r\n
            // lente). Deux rAF = première opportunité de peinture, page déjà\r\n
            // entièrement stylée par le CSS critique inliné.\r\n
            requestAnimationFrame(function() {\r\n
                requestAnimationFrame(dismiss);\r\n
            });\r\n
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', dismiss);\r\n
            window.addEventListener('load', dismiss);\r\n
            setTimeout(dismiss, 2500);\r\n
        })();\r\n

        <div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 p-6 flex flex-col items-center text-center">\r\n
            <div id="sh-score"></div>\r\n

            <p id="sh-meta" class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1"></p>\r\n
            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 w-full mt-4">\r\n
                                    <div class="rounded-lg bg-gray-50 p-2"><div id="sh-count-pass" class="text-xl font-bold text-green-700">0</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Réussis</div></div>\r\n
                                    <div class="rounded-lg bg-gray-50 p-2"><div id="sh-count-warn" class="text-xl font-bold text-orange-600">0</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">À améliorer</div></div>\r\n
                                    <div class="rounded-lg bg-gray-50 p-2"><div id="sh-count-fail" class="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">0</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">Échecs</div></div>\r\n

        <div class="p-4 border-b border-gray-200"><h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">En-têtes de réponse reçus</h2></div>\r\n

        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-4" id="tv-exports">\r\n

            <button type="button" data-tv-export="json"\r\n

        </div>\r\n
    </section>\r\n
\r\n
    <section class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 pb-12">\r\n
        <div class="space-y-8">\r\n
            <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-white rounded-2xl border-2 border-gray-200 p-6">\r\n
                <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-4">Comment Utiliser Cet Outil</h2>\r\n
                <div class="space-y-4">\r\n
                                            <div class="flex items-start gap-3">\r\n
                            <div\r\n
                                class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#00AEEF] text-white flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-sm font-bold">\r\n
                                1</div>\r\n
                            <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">Entrez l&#039;URL de votre page d&#039;accueil (ou de n&#039;importe quelle page : les en-têtes peuvent varier).</p>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="flex items-start gap-3">\r\n
                            <div\r\n
                                class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#00AEEF] text-white flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-sm font-bold">\r\n
                                2</div>\r\n
                            <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">Nous effectuons une requête et évaluons chaque en-tête de sécurité selon les recommandations OWASP et MDN.</p>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="flex items-start gap-3">\r\n
                            <div\r\n
                                class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#00AEEF] text-white flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 text-sm font-bold">\r\n
                                3</div>\r\n
                            <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">Copiez les directives proposées dans la configuration de votre serveur ou de votre CDN, puis relancez le test.</p>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                    </div>\r\n
            </div>\r\n
            <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-white border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-6 md:p-8">\r\n
                <div class="mb-6">\r\n
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                            height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"\r\n
                            stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                            class="lucide lucide-circle-question-mark w-5 h-5 text-[#00AEEF]" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                            <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle>\r\n
                            <path d="M9.09 9a3 3 0 0 1 5.83 1c0 2-3 3-3 3"></path>\r\n
                            <path d="M12 17h.01"></path>\r\n
                        </svg>\r\n
                        <h2 class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold text-black">Questions Fréquemment Posées</h2>\r\n
                    </div>\r\n
                    <p class="text-sm md:text-base text-gray-600">Pourquoi ces en-têtes comptent, pour la sécurité comme pour le SEO</p>\r\n
                </div>\r\n
                <div class="bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4 md:p-6">\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">1</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Les en-têtes de sécurité influencent-ils le référencement ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Indirectement. HTTPS est un signal de classement, et HSTS garantit qu&#039;il est utilisé. Un site compromis par XSS ou clickjacking peut être signalé comme dangereux par Google Safe Browsing, ce qui fait chuter le trafic. Enfin, ces en-têtes font partie des audits de confiance (E-E-A-T) menés par les agences et les acheteurs.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">2</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Qu&#039;est-ce que Strict-Transport-Security (HSTS) ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Il indique au navigateur de n&#039;utiliser que HTTPS pour ce domaine pendant une durée donnée. Sans lui, la première requête d&#039;un visiteur peut partir en HTTP et être interceptée. Une durée d&#039;un an (max-age=31536000) avec includeSubDomains est le standard.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">3</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Comment déployer une Content-Security-Policy sans tout casser ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Commencez en mode Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only avec un report-uri ou report-to : le navigateur signale les violations sans rien bloquer. Ajustez la politique jusqu&#039;à l&#039;absence de rapports, puis passez à Content-Security-Policy. Remplacez &#039;unsafe-inline&#039; par des nonces ou des hashes.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">4</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">X-Frame-Options ou frame-ancestors ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Les deux protègent du clickjacking. frame-ancestors (dans la CSP) est le standard actuel et permet d&#039;autoriser des domaines précis ; X-Frame-Options reste utile pour les anciens navigateurs. Les déclarer tous les deux est correct.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">5</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">Pourquoi signaler l&#039;en-tête Server ou X-Powered-By ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Ils révèlent le logiciel et sa version (nginx/1.18, PHP/8.1…), ce qui facilite la recherche de failles connues. Les masquer ne corrige rien mais réduit l&#039;exposition.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n
                        </div>\r\n
                                            <div class="border-b border-gray-200 last:border-0"><button\r\n
                                class="w-full py-6 flex items-start gap-4 text-left hover:bg-gray-50 -mx-4 px-4 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-200">\r\n
                                <div\r\n
                                    class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 bg-[#00AEEF]/10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">\r\n
                                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-[#00AEEF]">6</span>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">\r\n
                                    <h3 class="text-base md:text-lg font-semibold text-black">La note s&#039;applique-t-elle à tout le site ?</h3>\r\n
                                </div>\r\n
                                <div class="flex-shrink-0"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24"\r\n
                                        height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"\r\n
                                        stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"\r\n
                                        class="lucide lucide-chevron-down w-5 h-5 text-gray-400" aria-hidden="true">\r\n
                                        <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path>\r\n
                                    </svg></div>\r\n
                            </button>\r\n
                            <div class="faq-answer hidden px-4 pb-6 text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed"\r\n
                                style="padding-left:3.5rem">\r\n
                                <p>Non, à la page testée. Les en-têtes sont souvent définis globalement, mais un CDN, un sous-dossier ou une application peuvent les modifier. Testez les pages types : accueil, connexion, API, pages servies par un autre service.</p>\r\n
                            </div>\r\n

        (function() {\r\n
            var done = false;\r\n
\r\n
            function dismiss() {\r\n
                if (done) return;\r\n
                done = true;\r\n
                var p = document.getElementById('preloader');\r\n
                if (p) {\r\n
                    p.classList.add('loaded');\r\n
                    setTimeout(function() {\r\n
                        p.remove();\r\n
                    }, 600);\r\n
                }\r\n
            }\r\n
            // Ce script inline en fin de <body> s'exécute dès que le HTML est\r\n
            // parsé — AVANT le téléchargement des scripts defer (contrairement\r\n
            // à DOMContentLoaded, qui les attend : ~1,5 s de délai LCP en 4G\r\n
            // lente). Deux rAF = première opportunité de peinture, page déjà\r\n
            // entièrement stylée par le CSS critique inliné.\r\n
            requestAnimationFrame(function() {\r\n
                requestAnimationFrame(dismiss);\r\n
            });\r\n
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', dismiss);\r\n
            window.addEventListener('load', dismiss);\r\n
            setTimeout(dismiss, 2500);\r\n
        })();\r\n
    </script>\r\n
